  "pension_information": [
    [
      "Mandatory participation in Pensioenfonds Zorg en Welzijn (Article 2:1 lid 2 j): The employment contract must mention the mandatory participation in the Pensioenfonds Zorg en Welzijn."
    ],
    [
      "Pension scheme details (Article 2:10): The rights and obligations of employer and employee regarding the pension scheme, including the annual premium, are regulated by the pension regulations of Stichting Pensioenfonds Zorg en Welzijn. The pension premium distribution is 50% for the employer and 50% for the employee. The employee's share is recovered by the employer."
    ],
    [
      "Implementation of pension agreement: CAO parties will work on agreements for employees with demanding professions to retire earlier, before 1 June 2020."
    ],
    [
      "Parental leave and pension insurance (Article 8:21 lid 2, 3): The employee can voluntarily continue their pension insurance during parental leave. The employer covers a part of the premiums, provided the employee continues employment for at least 6 months after the unpaid leave. The employer's share is, if legally possible, equal to the employer's contribution immediately preceding the unpaid leave, but not more than the total premiums owed to the pension fund during the unpaid leave."
    ],
    [
      "PBL and pension premium (Article 8A:5 lid 3): For PBL from the life-course savings scheme used to finance unpaid leave, the employer pays the employer's contribution of the pension premium."

  "leave_information": [
    [
      "Vacation rights (Article 8:1): The employee has with retention of salary right to 144 vacation hours per calendar year. For part-time employees, this is pro-rata. For employees with average working hours above 1878 hours per year, the number of vacation hours is also pro-rata. Per 1 January 2016, for employees in job group 65 or lower, the hourly wage over taken statutory vacation hours is increased by the average paid irregular shift allowance (ORT) over the six months prior to the vacation month."
    ],
    [
      "Reduction of vacation rights (Article 8:3): Vacation duration is reduced proportionally if the employee starts or ends employment during the calendar year, or works part-time. The entitlement after reduction is rounded up to whole hours if necessary."
    ],
    [
      "Taking vacation (Article 8:6): Vacation is granted according to the employee's wishes, uninterrupted if desired, unless department or service interests prevent it. The employee can claim at least 3 consecutive weeks of vacation, including preceding and succeeding weekends. The general regulation and spread of vacation within the institution requires works council/employee representation consent."
    ],
    [
      "Mandatory collective closure (Article 8:7): For employees in day care centers, if a closure regulation is established, employees must take vacation days during closure periods, unless work prevents it."
    ],
    [
      "Withdrawal of vacation (Article 8:8): Granted vacation can be withdrawn if the institution's interest urgently requires it. Financial damage suffered by the employee due to withdrawal is compensated. If an employee is demonstrably incapacitated for work (Art. 7:629 BW) during scheduled vacation, those days are not considered vacation days."
    ],
    [
      "Taking vacation in calendar year (Article 8:9): Vacation should generally be taken in the relevant calendar year. In exceptional situations, employer and employee can agree to deviate."
    ],
    [
      "Conversion of vacation to money (Article 8:10): Except as per Article 7:641 BW, vacation rights are not converted into a monetary payment."
    ],
    [
      "Definitions of leave (Article 8:12): Paid leave means hours in a work schedule/roster where no work is required, counted towards total working hours. Unpaid leave means the right to be free from all duties. For this chapter, children for whom an adoption application has been submitted are considered the employee's children. For part-time employees, the pro-rata principle applies."
    ],
    [
      "Unpaid leave for special events (Article 8:13): The employer grants unpaid leave for: employee's relocation; employee's marriage notification; registration of registered partnership; notarized deed establishing unmarried cohabitation; attending Provincial States, Municipal Council, Regional Council, or Water Board meetings as a member; preparing for an exam in the training agreement (max 4 half days or 2 full days per year directly before the exam)."
    ],
    [
      "Days off for special events (Article 8:14): The employer grants paid leave for: employee's relocation on employer's instruction (2 days); marriage or registered partnership of a family member (1 day); marriage or registered partnership of blood relatives in the first and second degree of the employee, spouse, or relationship partner (1 day); employee's 25th and 40th wedding anniversary (1 day); 25th, 40th, 50th, and 60th wedding anniversary of employee's parents or foster parents, spouse, or relationship partner (1 day); employee's 25th and 40th service jubilee (1 day); death of spouse or relationship partner, (foster) children, (foster) parents of employee or spouse/relationship partner (day of death until day of burial or cremation); death of or attending burial/cremation of blood relatives in the second degree of employee, spouse, or relationship partner (1 day). For some events, employee must notify 14 days in advance. The pro-rata principle does not apply to part-timers for these specific events. The employer grants 24 hours paid leave per year for illness in the family requiring care, with a regulation established with the works council."
    ],
    [
      "Leave for committee membership and similar (Article 8:15): The employer allows the employee to participate in administrative and ministerial committees and disciplinary tribunals in healthcare, granting paid leave if it leads to absence from scheduled working hours. The employer also allows participation in meetings of public law bodies where the employee is elected/appointed. The employee can receive paid or unpaid leave for these activities. If paid leave is chosen, the employer claims compensation the employee receives from the function for which leave is granted. The choice is determined annually in consultation with the employer."
    ],
    [
      "Marriage and cohabitation (Article 8:16): An employee entering marriage or other cohabitation is entitled to 2 extra PBL days (= 14.4 hours). The employer grants these extra days only once for the same cohabitation. For assistant doctors in training and employees with an average of 38 hours, 16 hours apply instead of 14.4 hours."
    ],
    [
      "Other leave possibilities (Article 8:17): The employer can grant paid or unpaid leave for other special events not mentioned in the CAO, at the employer's discretion."
    ],
    [
      "Unpaid extension of maternity leave (Article 8:18): Following paid (maternity) leave, the employer grants the employee, if desired, unpaid leave for a maximum of 4 weeks. Employer and employee must agree on this leave at least 3 months before the expected delivery date."
    ],
    [
      "Palliative leave (Article 8:19): For very serious illness of a spouse/relationship partner, (foster) parents, (foster) child, or a relative living with the employee, requiring home nursing and/or care, the employee taking on care receives paid leave for a continuous period of a maximum of 3 months after consulting the employer. Vacation leave accrued during this period is included. A doctor's/practitioner's statement is required."
    ],
    [
      "Refusal of leave (Article 8:20): If, from the perspective of institution/client interest, the employer cannot reasonably be expected to allow absence or grant leave at a specific time, a (partial) refusal can be made after consulting the employee."
    ],
    [
      "Parental leave (Article 8:21): The employee is entitled to parental leave until the child reaches 8 years of age, as per the Wet Arbeid en Zorg. The employee can voluntarily continue pension and/or health insurance, with the employer covering part of the premiums if employment continues for at least 6 months after unpaid leave. The employer's share is equal to the contribution immediately preceding unpaid leave, but not more than the total premiums owed. The employee receives 25% of the salary for parental leave taken in the child's first year of life. If employment ends during or within 6 months after parental leave (or reduction of working hours) at the employee's request, the employee must repay the paid salary for taken parental leave. No vacation leave is accrued during parental leave hours."
    ],
    [
      "Personal Life Phase Budget (PBL) (Article 8A:1): The employee receives, in addition to statutory vacation hours, a PBL of 57 hours per calendar year on a full-time basis, accrued monthly. This is pro-rata for partial years. The PBL can be taken as leave, saved in time, or paid out. Eligible employees can convert PBL to money for a life-course savings account. The employee makes their choice before the calendar year."
    ],
    [
      "Principles of Personal Life Phase Budget (Article 8A:2): The PBL facilitates sustainable employability. The employee makes an annual plan for PBL use, discussed in the annual review. The employer administers PBL for time saving and provides an annual overview. PBL is accrued for the first six months of sickness/incapacity. The employee can request to buy a maximum of 7 extra PBL days (50.4 hours, pro-rata for part-timers) for use in the year of purchase (not for sale). Accrued PBL can be paid out at the end of a calendar year upon employee request, unless compelling business interest prevents it. Remaining PBL is paid out upon employment termination/death. PBL accrued over worked hours of on-call workers and overtime of part-timers is paid as a surcharge on hourly wage, unless otherwise agreed."
    ],
    [
      "Taking PBL (Article 8A:3): PBL can be taken at the employee's discretion on a first-in, first-out basis. For substantial use, a written request must be submitted at least four months in advance. For non-substantial use, a written request within a reasonable period. The employer grants the request unless a compelling business interest, where the employee's interest must yield. If PBL leave and sickness coincide, PBL leave is suspended for the duration of the sickness. A new end date is agreed upon, and a doctor's statement may be requested."
    ],
    [
      "Saving PBL (Article 8A:4): PBL can be saved entirely or partially in time with the employer, value-indexed for a maximum of five years against the current hourly wage. If not taken within five years, it is paid out at the end of the calendar year after the indexation period, unless employer and employee agree otherwise for sustainable employability, in which case it does not expire."
    ],
    [
      "Saving PBL via life-course savings scheme (Article 8A:5): Eligible employees can convert accrued PBL to money once a year at the end of the calendar year for deposit into a life-course savings account (based on current hourly wage plus holiday allowance). New PBL is accrued pro-rata for PBL used to finance unpaid leave. The employer pays the employer's contribution to pension and IZZ health insurance premiums for PBL used to finance unpaid leave. In case of sickness/incapacity longer than seven days during PBL leave, the remaining PBL is suspended. The employer ensures re-staffing for taken PBL hours."
    ],
    [
      "PBL transitional law for employees aged 50-64 on 31 December 2009 (Article 8A:7): In deviation from Article 8A:1 paragraph 1, employees who on 31 December 2009 were 50-54 years old receive an annual PBL of 102-172 hours (depending on age) from the first day of the month they turn 55. Employees aged 55 but not yet 65 receive an annual PBL of 206 hours."
    ],
    [
      "PBL transitional law for employees aged 45-49 on 31 December 2009 (Article 8A:8): In addition to Article 8A:1, employees aged 45-49 on 31 December 2009 with 10 years of continuous service in the healthcare sector (PFZW scope) and continuous employment with the same employer until turning 55, receive a one-off PBL deposit of 200 hours (pro-rata) in the month they turn 55. Notification to the employer before 1 December 2009 is required to verify service years."
    ],
    [
      "Facilities for members of employee organizations (Article 14:2): A member of a CAO employee organization is entitled to 1 day of leave per calendar year for attending activities organized by the employee organization, if participation occurs during scheduled working hours. The employee must show the invitation upon employer request."
    ],
    [
      "Facilities for employee organization board members (Article 14:3): A board member is entitled to 2 training days per calendar year for courses organized by the employee organization. A board member is allowed to participate in meetings of statutory or regulatory bodies of employee organizations for a total of 264 hours per year, if participation occurs during scheduled working hours."

  "termination_information": [
    [
      "End of employment contract (Article 2:5): The employment contract ends by mutual consent; when the employee reaches AOW-eligible age; by operation of law upon expiry of the term or completion of work; for indefinite contracts, by termination with observance of Article 2:6; by termination for urgent reasons (Art. 7:678 and 7:679 BW); by dissolution by the subdistrict court (Art. 7:671b or 7:671c BW); immediately, by unilateral termination during a written agreed probationary period; by operation of law upon the employee's death; on the first day of the next calendar month, if the employee wishing to terminate due to childbirth notifies the employer in writing no later than 10 calendar days after childbirth; at the time of using the bridging benefit under the Pensioenfonds Zorg en Welzijn pension scheme, unless employee and employer agree otherwise in writing. If the contract ends due to reaching AOW-eligible age, it ends on the day before, unless another date is agreed."
    ],
    [
      "Termination (Article 2:6): In case of termination (Art. 2:5 lid 1 d) or if a fixed-term contract includes a clause for early termination, statutory notice periods apply, unless explicitly stated otherwise in this CAO. Employer termination must be in writing. If the notice period for the employee is extended, the notice period for employee and employer is equal if not longer than 3 months. If longer than 3 months, the employer's notice period is twice as long as the employee's. The effective date of dismissal is the first day of the calendar month."
    ],
    [
      "Suspension (Op non-actiefstelling) (Article 2:7): The employer can suspend the employee for a maximum of 3 weeks if work progress is seriously hindered. This term can be extended by a maximum of 6 weeks after consultation. Longer extensions are possible if causes are outside the employer's control. After a dismissal procedure has been initiated, the suspension can be extended until the employment contract ends or the dismissal procedure is concluded. The decision to suspend or extend must be communicated in writing with reasons. Suspension is always with retention of all other rights. The employer must promote the employee's return to work. Suspension cannot be used as a disciplinary measure."
    ],
    [
      "Disciplinary suspension (Schorsing) (Article 2:8): The employer can suspend the employee for a maximum of one week with salary retention for serious reasons. The suspension must be immediately, motivated, and confirmed by registered letter. The employee must be given the opportunity to account for themselves within 4 days. The employer can extend the suspension once by a maximum of one week. If a dismissal procedure has been initiated, the suspension can be extended until the dismissal effective date. In case of extension, the employee retains their salary. If the employee was wrongly suspended, the employer must publicly rehabilitate and compensate demonstrable damage."
    ],
    [
      "Death benefit (Article 2:9): In case of the employee's death, the employer provides a benefit based on the last earned salary for the period from the day after death until the last day of the third full calendar month after the month of death to the spouse/relationship partner, or failing that, minor children, or failing that, a person largely supported by the employee and living in a family context. If no such relation, the employer can grant the benefit (or part) to person(s) deemed eligible on grounds of fairness. The death benefit is reduced by any death benefit from statutory insurance."
    ],
    [
      "Evaluation CAO-regulation compensation upon dismissal: Parties will evaluate Chapter 15 (Compensation upon dismissal) before 1 January 2021, considering its intended purpose, use, and effect. Based on this, continuation, adjustment, or termination will be discussed."
    ],

The employee has a right to a supplement to the transition payment if the conditions of Article 15:2 are met (guarantee right). A portion of the transition payment and/or guarantee right is protected as income provision, from which costs for transition and employability cannot be deducted (Article 15:4)."
    ],

For calculation, continuous full years of service with the employer and continuous full months of service with other employers under CAO Gehandicaptenzorg immediately preceding current employment (rounded to full years) are counted. Employer indemnifies employee and waives recourse, except for intentional or deliberate recklessness."], ["Compensation for damage to employee: Material damage caused by a client, which could not reasonably be prevented, is reimbursed by the employer based on replacement value, up to a maximum of €2269 per event. This includes damage to goods and recovery/permanent disability costs (max 24 months). Employee must prove client caused damage, no other compensation, damage occurred during duty, and adequate insurance for usual risks."], ["Legal assistance: Employer provides adequate legal assistance if employee is involved in internal/external complaint procedure (including disciplinary law), unless negligence or deliberate recklessness. If not established, employer provides assistance. If negligence/recklessness is later proven, employer can recover costs. Employer informs and consults on legal consequences. Works council has right of consent (WOR Article 27). Government funds for workload reduction spent after consultation with works council. OAGz decides within 16 weeks based on severe circumstances, participation in OAGz-designated experiment, and guaranteed employee conditions. Calculated as 'ORT paid in the calendar years 2012 to 2015 x 8.3%'. Paid no later than the last quarter of 2017. Employer and employee can agree to spread the payment over several years."], ["Salary definition: The agreed gross monthly salary between employer and employee, excluding the year-end bonus, holiday allowances, and the reimbursements mentioned in chapters 4, 7, 8, 9 and 10."], ["Hourly wage definition: The amount obtained by multiplying the salary as defined in Article 1:1 sub d, for a full-time employment, by 12 and dividing by 1878. (For a 38-hour per week contract, divide by 1983, and for a 40-hour per week contract, divide by 2087 hours)."], ["Job evaluation FWG: Functions are classified into groups 5-80 using FWG 3.0 (until April 1, 2018) or FWG Gehandicaptenzorg (from April 1, 2018). Exceptions for WML-exempt employees."], ["General salary: Salary scales in Article 4:15 apply to full-time employment (1878 hours/year). Part-time salary is pro-rata. For employees working >1878 hours/year, salary is adjusted by factor (hours of employment/1878). Trainee salaries are in Chapter 5."], ["Classification in the functional scale: Employer determines functional salary scale based on job classification (Article 4:1). Scale number corresponds to job group number."], ["Application of salary scales: Employee is placed at 0 functional years or higher based on experience. Employees 23+ years old placed below minimum wage are moved to the next higher insertion number above minimum wage within the scale after max one year."], ["Year-end bonus: Employees employed on Dec 31 receive a year-end bonus of 7.05% of actual salary earned from Jan 1 to Dec 31. If employment ends before Dec 31, pro-rata bonus is paid. Only one year-end bonus per calendar year."], ["Salary Payment: Salary paid by 2 days before end of calendar month. Reimbursements from chapters 7 and 9 paid by end of following calendar month. Written notification of salary changes."], ["Periodic increases: Annual salary increase within functional scale, unless otherwise agreed. First increase 1 year after employment or promotion. Employer can grant no or multiple increases based on performance review system (transparent, appealable, allows consultation for negative reviews)."], ["Special allowances: Employer can grant a gratuity. Temporary allowance up to 10% of salary for specific functions with labor market shortages. Allowance for special circumstances, max 8 consecutive scale numbers above current salary, not exceeding max of next higher functional scale."], ["Holiday allowance: 8% of actual salary earned from June 1 to May 31 for employees employed for a full year on May 31. Minimum calculated on insertion number 8, pro-rata. Paid once a year in May. Pro-rata for partial year employment or termination before May 31. Not mandatory for fixed-term contracts."], ["Promotion: Upon promotion to a higher job group, salary is increased by 2 periodic increments within the new scale, at least the minimum of the new scale, but not exceeding its maximum. Employer can deviate with works council consent. Applies to AVGs after SBOH training."], ["Acting up: Employee acting up in a higher-graded function for >1 month continuously (not vacation replacement) receives compensation equal to difference between current salary and starting salary of higher function, plus minimum 2 periodic increments. Max is the higher function's scale maximum. Pro-rata for at least half of daily working hours. Employer can deviate with works council consent."], ["Jubilee gratuity: One-time gratuity for uninterrupted service: 1/4 monthly salary at 12.5 years, 1/2 at 25 years, full monthly salary at 40 years. Upon termination due to AOW-eligible age, 1/2 monthly salary."], ["Reclassification and salary consequences: If reclassification results in a lower job group, salary is frozen if it exceeds max salary of lower group + 10%, adjusted for general wage increases. Employee can reach max salary + 10% of lower group. If higher job group, new scale applies, salary is at least current salary or next higher amount in new scale, at least minimum of new scale."], ["Salary scale for trainees: Trainees (qualification level 2, 3, 4 or 5) based on 1878 hours/year full-time. For level 5, second year can be theoretical with stages, then third year starts at second practical year salary. Employer can agree higher salary for trainees 21+ (or younger with relevant diploma) if relevant diploma, labor market shortage, or re-entrant. Requires OR/PVT agreement. Trainee nurses IG (level 3) and nurses (level 4) receive gross pocket money equal to minimum (youth) wage during vocational preparatory period. No pocket money if receiving equal or higher benefit. Pro-rata for part-time. Salary based on actual working hours."], ["Continued wage payment during sickness: Employee unable to work due to sickness, pregnancy, childbirth (Article 7:629 BW) receives: 100% gross monthly wage for 52 weeks, then 70% for subsequent 52 weeks. Gross monthly wage includes: gross monthly salary at onset of disability, other paid structural wage components (e.g., on-call, irregular service bonuses, measured monthly over 6 months prior). Wage adjusted for periodic increases, general salary adjustments, agreed changes in working hours/contract, fiscal/social security/pension premium changes. Employee retains holiday allowance, reduced by statutory/PFZW benefits. Not applicable during pregnancy/maternity leave (Wet Arbeid en Zorg Article 3:1). Wage reduced by statutory benefits and income from other work. Periods of disability combined if interruption <4 weeks or directly before/after pregnancy/maternity leave, unless different cause. If disability due to employer's liability (Article 7:658 lid 2 BW), 100% wage paid for 104 weeks. Employer can pay >70% in second year in individual cases. Right to continued wage payment ends with employment."], ["Productive work and reintegration activities during sickness: For hours worked in suitable work or work without wage value (e.g., therapeutic work, training, internship) during second 52-week period (Article 11:4 lid 1 sub b), employee receives 100% of gross monthly wage at onset of disability. Other hours paid per Article 11:4 lid 1 sub b and lid 2. Total gross income (lid 1 and 2) max 100% of gross wage (Article 11:4 lid 1 sub a and lid 2), never less than 70%."], ["Wage supplement early IVA benefit: If employee receives IVA benefit during first 52 weeks of sickness (Article 11:4 lid 1 sub a), employer supplements to 100% of gross wage (Article 11:4 lid 2). This ends after 52 weeks. Gross wage reduced by statutory/PFZW benefits and income from other work."], ["Disability of 0-35%: Employee with <35% disability after 2 years of sickness (WIA claim assessment) receives 100% salary for hours worked in current function or at same salary level. If new function with different salary, receives that salary for hours worked. The Schoenen module applies to a company that falls under the scope of article 1 and whose turnover from 1.c is greater than each of the other parts of the turnover and greater than the cumulative turnover from 1.f, g, h, i, j and 2 to 6 for Wonen. The Tuincentra module applies to a company that falls under the scope of article 1 and whose turnover from 1.e is greater than each of the other parts of the turnover and greater than the cumulative turnover from 1.f, g, h, i, j and 2 to 6 for Wonen."], ["Exclusions from scope: This CAO does not apply to the employment contract of the employer with: statutory directors; employees who perform short-term non-professional work, including holiday workers; employees employed by IKEA or de Bijenkorf."], ["Exclusions by other CAOs: The CAO also does not apply to the employment contract of the employer and employee who fall under the scope of one of the following CAOs: the KNDB CAO; the Audax- or Bruna CAO; the CAO for the Book and Office Trade; the CAO Specialized Retail in Flowers and Plants; the CAO Food Industry and furthermore to employment contracts between an employer and an employee in which the CAO Food Industry has been declared applicable."], ["Further exclusions: Furthermore, the CAO does not apply to the employment contract between the employer and the employee, who works in a company that mainly sells pets, goods for the care, feeding and housing of pets, as well as fishing articles to private individuals."], ["Deviation from CAO: The employer may not agree to terms of employment that conflict with the provisions of the CAO, unless dispensation has been granted for this. The employer can request dispensation from the application of (a) provision(s) of the CAO. See article 17 of this CAO. The employer may offer terms of employment that deviate positively from the CAO for the employee. For certain articles, it is possible to make deviating agreements in consultation with the works council, which are at least equivalent in level to the agreements in the CAO."], ["Definitions - Loon: The financial compensation agreed between employer and employee for performing the agreed work. For every employee, the following is included in the wage: the period or basic (hourly) wage; the holiday allowance; diploma allowance; the commission related to individual performance; fixed allowances. This also includes the allowance for working at certain hours, if the employee regularly works at these hours. (*01 = It is not intended that holiday allowance is paid over holiday allowance.)"], ["Definitions - Garantieloon: The amount that must be paid to the employee at least based on the wage scales in this CAO."], ["Definitions - Basis- or contracturen: The contractually agreed average number of working hours per week. For flexible deployment, this average is the benchmark for determining the bandwidth. See also article 4.2 and 5.2 of this CAO."], ["Definitions - Fulltime employment: An employee whose agreed working hours are 38 hours per week and in Wonen 37 hours per week. If, based on the individual employment contract, the agreed working hours are less or more than the working hours of the full-time employee, the provisions of this CAO apply proportionally to the individual working hours, unless otherwise stated in the relevant articles. The employer and employee can agree on working hours of 40 hours per week if the employee agrees."], ["Definitions - Gerealiseerde uren: Realized hours include: all actually worked hours (see article 4.2 of this CAO); paid sick leave hours; taken and paid vacation hours; taken and paid special leave; compensation of allowances in time; and other compensation in hours existing at the individual companies."], ["Definitions - WML: Wettelijk Minimumloon (Statutory Minimum Wage)."], ["Definitions - Feestdagen (Public Holidays): New Year's Day; both Easter days; Ascension Day; both Pentecost days; both Christmas days; the day designated by the government on which King's Day is celebrated; lustrum celebration of Liberation Day (once every 5 years in years that are a multiple of 5)."], ["Definitions - Werkrooster: A working time arrangement that indicates at what times the individual employee starts, interrupts and ends his work. See also article 5 of this CAO."], ["Wage specification: The employer provides a wage specification, possibly digitally, to the employee for each wage payment. This specification must at least indicate: name of the employer and employee; the amount of the wage; the period over which the wage was earned; the composition of the wage and any allowances; the deductions applied to the wage; the statutory minimum hourly wage for the employee's age; the agreed working hours."], ["Confidentiality: The employee keeps confidential those matters for which confidentiality has been imposed on him, or of which he should reasonably understand that confidentiality is expected. This applies both during and after the employment contract."], ["Relocation: The employer can, in consultation, relocate the employee to another branch of the employer for reasons of business interest. If the relocation is temporary, this happens after consultation with the employee. In case of temporary relocation, this can also be a branch of a company affiliated with the employer, provided that it also falls under the scope of this CAO. Relocation is possible, unless it cannot reasonably be demanded of the employee."], ["Closure of branch: In the context of closing a branch, the employer will inform the employees of the branch as early as possible, and at least 1 month before the closure, about the closure of the branch. If in this situation the employment contract of the employee ends at the initiative of the employer, the employment contract ends at least after observing the statutory notice period."], ["Written agreement: The employer offers the employee a written employment contract upon commencement of employment. This must at least state: the name of the employer and employee; the date of commencement of employment; the duration of the employment contract; the function; the wage agreed with the employee; the agreed working hours/basic hours; the place where the work is usually performed; that this CAO applies."], ["Chain rule for temporary workers: If an employer hires a former temporary worker who immediately prior to this worked as a temporary worker for the employer, then the different temporary employment contracts are considered as one employment contract for the combined duration of the temporary employment contracts. The employee who, after having worked as a temporary worker for the employer, immediately enters into employment with the employer, therefore starts the second chain contract."], ["All-in agreements: It can be agreed with the employee that so-called “all-in” agreements are made for him. In that case, it is possible that terms of employment mentioned in this CAO are compensated in (an) other term(s) of employment."], ["Flexible deployment - Basisuren: Employer and employee can agree on flexible deployment in the employment contract. In that case, the employment contract states how many hours the employee works on average per week and, as of 1 July 2022, that flexible deployment applies. These are the contract hours or basic hours and serve as the starting point for flexible working."], ["Flexible deployment - Bandwidth: If flexible working has been agreed, the employer can oblige the employee to work weekly more or fewer hours within a bandwidth of + and - 30 percent of the number of basic hours. In any case, a minimum bandwidth of + and - 6 hours relative to the number of basic hours is possible and a maximum number of scheduled hours of 45 hours per week."], ["Flexible deployment - Referteperiode: The employer sets a period of 12 months as the basis for determining the extent to which the actual working time deviates from the number of basic hours. This period is called the reference period. Reference periods may not overlap."], ["Flexible deployment - Actual working time: For the determination of whether the employee has been deployed in accordance with his contract hours, different types of hours count: the so-called realized hours, see article 2. Any mandatory presence of the employee required by the employer must be considered as working time, with the exception of an incidental and unplanned limited exceeding of the scheduled hours. Such an incidental and/or limited exceeding does not count as realized hours and is therefore not paid."], ["Flexible deployment - More hours worked than basic hours: If at the end of a reference period more hours have been worked than the number of basic hours calculated for the reference period, the employer pays the employee for the extra hours worked. Plus hours worked in the reference period do not automatically lead to an adjustment of the number of contract hours agreed in the individual employment contract."], ["Flexible deployment - Fewer hours worked than basic hours: If at the end of a reference period fewer hours have been worked than the number of basic hours calculated for the reference period, these minus hours lapse and the employee does not have to make them up by working more in the subsequent period."], ["Flexible deployment - Deviating agreements: Deviating agreements can be made between the employer and the individual employee from the provisions of article 4.2 of this CAO. See also article 2 (Definitions - basic hours)."], ["End of employment due to age: The employment contract ends without notice on the day the AOW-eligible age is reached."], ["Five-day work week: The employee cannot be obliged to work more than five days per week."], ["Evening work: A retail employee cannot be obliged to work more than two evenings per week, except in December. For non-retail personnel, a maximum of three evenings per week applies, and an average of two evenings per week over a six-month period. With written consent from the works council, the employer may deviate from these maximums for non-retail personnel, provided the applicable compensation in money or time remains at least equal."], ["Work schedules: When establishing the work schedule, the employer will take the employee's wishes into account as much as possible, unless this cannot reasonably be demanded of the employer. The employee must inform the employer as much as possible in a timely manner of special circumstances, for example regarding childcare, care tasks or care for the sick, so that the employer takes this into account as much as possible in personnel planning."], ["Non-schedulable days: An employee with a contractual working week of 20 hours or less is entitled to a maximum of two non-schedulable days per week. The employee must submit the request in writing two months before the desired start date, or in the case of a new employee, before starting employment. The employer can in principle not refuse a request, unless there are compelling business interests, which must be demonstrated by the employer. The employer indicates in writing as soon as possible, but no later than one month before the desired start date, whether the request will be honored. If the employer has not responded within this period, the request is deemed to have been honored. For an employee with a contractual working week greater than 20 hours per week, the foregoing also applies, with the understanding that this concerns a maximum of 1 non-schedulable day per week. Saturdays and Sundays are excluded from the non-schedulable days to be agreed upon. The possibility to indicate non-schedulable days does not apply to on-call workers."], ["Right to roster-free days per month: For employees working on Saturdays and Sundays, the following applies: To prevent employees from having to work all Saturdays and Sundays, they are entitled, upon request, to one roster-free Saturday or Sunday per month. As of this date, employees also have the right, upon request, to not work two consecutive days once a month. These two consecutive days are planned in consultation between the employee and the employer."], ["Work schedule announcement: The work schedule will be established and announced at least 3 weeks in advance in consultation with the individual employee, except for an on-call employee. The 3-week period can be deviated from in exceptional cases (*02). (*02 = This refers to major personnel problems, unforeseen circumstances beyond the employer's control.)"], ["Right to a free weekend: An employee's request to the employer for one free weekend every three months will be granted, provided they are willing to work a full weekend during that period."], ["Split shifts: An employee may not work a split shift. This is the case if there is an interruption of more than one hour during working hours, excluding lunch, coffee break and dinner."], ["Unreachability: CAO parties attach importance to a healthy work-life balance. The starting point is that the employee is unreachable outside the individually agreed working hours (schedule). If there are special, urgent circumstances that require reachability, the employee and supervisor make agreements about this in advance. Employees are not obliged to follow their employer's communication in their free time."], ["Normal working hours: The normal working hours are the times when the company may be open according to the Shop Opening Hours Act. For working at certain hours, an allowance may apply (article 7.1 to article 7.4 of this CAO). The normal working hours for a full-time employee are an average of 38 hours per week, calculated over a period of 12 consecutive months. For employees in Wonen, full-time employment is an average of 37 hours per week."], ["Overtime definition: Overtime occurs when the employee, after consultation between the employer and the employee, has been instructed to perform work: for more than 10 hours per day; or for more than 40 hours per week, calculated over a period of four consecutive weeks. The pattern of the four-week periods is the same for all employees in the company. The employer prevents overtime as much as possible, but can legally demand it from the employee. The employee is then obliged to perform the work. In this case, the employee is entitled to an overtime allowance. See article 7.4. In the case of plannable and/or foreseeable work, an incidental exceeding of the aforementioned hour limits by fifteen minutes or less is not considered overtime. The overtime limits apply to all employees. An employee in full-time employment with a gross wage per month higher than twice the statutory gross minimum wage per month (for employees aged 21 and older) is not eligible for overtime allowance. Overtime hours worked only count once for determining whether overtime has occurred."], ["Working conditions - RI&E: There is a Risk Inventory & Evaluation (RI&E) for the sector. Employers who use this recognized sector RI&E and are enabled by the legislator to suffice with a lighter, or no, assessment, are not obliged to have the RI&E assessed (see www.rie.nl and www.arboportaal.nl)."], ["Working conditions - Safety: BBL students, employees younger than 20 years and employees in function group A may not work alone in the store premises, with the exception of breaks."], ["Collective health insurance: Employees can take out health insurance for their partner, child(ren) and themselves on INretail's contract with its health insurer (or the insurer with whom a contract will be concluded in the future)."], ["CAO term: This CAO runs from 1 January 2024 to 31 December 2025."], ["Old or other regulations: Upon the entry into force of this CAO, all previous terms of employment regulations of CAO Retail Non-Food, including CAO Tuincentra, of whatever nature, lose their effect, with the exception of company regulations insofar as they do not conflict with this CAO."], ["CAO compliance and Social Committee: In case of interpretation differences regarding correct compliance with or application for dispensation from (an aspect/article in) the CAO, an employee and/or an employer, or their authorized representative(s), can turn to the Social Committee."], ["Social Committee composition: The Social Committee has 7 (and 6 substitute) members. 3 members (and 3 substitutes) are chosen by the employee organization(s). The employer organizations also choose 3 members (and 3 substitutes). To complete, employee and employer organizations choose a seventh independent member. This last member is also chairman of the committee. INretail appoints a secretary who serves the Social Committee, but is not a member of the committee. The secretary's office is at Arnhemse Bovenweg 100, 3708 AG Zeist."], ["Social Committee tasks: Granting dispensation from (an aspect/article in) this CAO at the written request of the employer or employee; Providing a binding advice in case of an interpretation difference regarding the explanation and/or application of this CAO, when employer and employee declare in advance to accept that advice; Providing requested and unrequested advice in case of an ambiguity regarding the application of an article."], ["Social Committee working method for interpretation differences: After written submission of a unilateral request by an employee or an employer, the applicant and the counterparty are asked for written consent to receive a binding advice. After both parties' consent, the counterparty is asked to provide their substantive view on the request. The applicant and the counterparty may, if desirable for the committee members' understanding, be asked to provide additional information on the request or view based on both parties' positions. The Social Committee will reach an understanding, opinion and decision through internal consultation and possibly a hearing. The applicant and the counterparty will both be informed of the binding advice in a uniform written manner. If the employer and/or employee do not agree in advance to binding advice, the Social Committee will provide written advice to the applicant and send a copy to the counterparty. During the procedure, the parties, being employer and employee, are bound by a response period of a maximum of 4 weeks."], ["Social Committee dispensation: The Social Committee can also be asked by the employer and/or employee to grant dispensation from one or more articles/provisions from the CAO. The Social Committee will in any case test the following points in a dispensation request: The reasons why dispensation is requested must be sufficiently substantiated; The terms of employment for the employees must be sufficiently guaranteed; The terms of employment scheme(s) proposed by the employer may not conflict with legal provisions. The Social Committee will, within 2 weeks of receipt of a sufficiently described, motivated and documented request, or within 2 weeks of receipt of the requested additional information, inform the applicant that the request will be processed. The Social Committee will make a decision as soon as possible, but no later than 8 weeks after the request has been processed. This period of 8 weeks can be extended once by 8 weeks. The decision will be communicated to the applicant in writing and with reasons."]], "wage_information": [["Wage specification: The employer provides a wage specification, possibly digitally, to the employee for each wage payment. This is agreed between employer and employee before the start of flexible working and recorded in the individual employment contract. The wage per hour worked can never be lower than the Statutory Minimum Hourly Wage."], ["Flexible deployment - Payment of extra hours upon termination: If upon termination it appears that the employee has worked more hours than for which he has received wages, these hours will be paid out. These plus hours are paid out at the normal wage applicable to the employee (including holiday allowance)."], ["Flexible deployment - Settlement of minus hours upon termination: At the end of the employment contract, remaining minus hours will be settled in the final settlement, unless they were caused by circumstances within the employer's sphere of influence."], ["Function classification: This CAO uses a job evaluation system (FUWAM, based on the CATS methodology). There are 9 function levels, A to I, and corresponding wage scales. Each function level contains full-fledged functions; each scale can therefore be an end scale. In 2020, the job evaluation system was expanded with E-business functions. Employees are classified at the correct function level using the Retail Non-Food Job Classification Handbook (formerly: Fashion, Sport & Lifestyle) or the online tools provided by the industry organization, such as the website www.functiewijzer.nl. Appendix 1c contains a summary of the level sheets of that Handbook. The Handbook is available digitally. For employees who do not agree with the classification, there is an objection and appeal procedure (see appendix 1d). The employee receives a wage that is at least equal to the amount determined for his age or for his function years according to the wage scales (see also appendix 1b of this CAO). The amounts included therein are gross and are stated based on an hour worked."], ["Determination of monthly wage for employees at WML level: In accordance with article 11 paragraph 1 WML, at least the minimum hourly wage applicable in that month must be paid to the employee over the average number of working hours per month. For calculating the monthly remuneration for a fixed agreed weekly working time, the following formula applies: (the number of working hours in a calendar year divided by 12) times the minimum hourly wage. This formula also applies to part-timers."], ["Determination of monthly wage for employees above WML: For the calculation of the full-time monthly wage, the hourly wage is multiplied by 164.67. For Wonen, for the calculation of the full-time monthly wage, the hourly wage is multiplied by 160.33."], ["Wage adjustment on 1 January 2024: On 1 January 2024, the scale wages and the wages of employees who earn no more than the old maximum of their scale will be increased. There is no single percentage by which wages are increased. As of 1 January 2024, new wage tables apply to all branches, in which the hourly wages are stated. The scales and steps have different wage increase percentages, compared to the wage tables as of 1 July 2023. The different wage increase percentages are stated with the wage tables as of 1 January 2024. The minimum wage increase for employees in the wage tables is 3.75%, the percentage by which the Statutory Minimum Wage will be increased on 1 January 2024."], ["Wage adjustment for employees above old maximum on 1 January 2024: On 1 January 2024, the wages of employees who earn more than the old maximum of their scale, but less than the new, will be increased. The employee's wage will be increased by the increase percentage belonging to the highest step in the white section. If, with the above calculation, the employee again earns more than the new maximum of the scale, and is therefore above scale, the increase will be capped at this new maximum."], ["Wage adjustment on 1 July 2024, 1 January 2025 and 1 July 2025: On 1 July 2024, 1 January 2025 and 1 July 2025, the scale wages and the wages of employees who earn no more than the old maximum of their scale will be increased by the indexation percentage by which the Statutory Minimum Wage is increased on those dates. On the same dates, the wages of employees who at that time earn more than the old maximum of their scale, but less than the new, will be increased by that indexation percentage, unless the new maximum of the scale is exceeded. In that case, the new maximum of the scale becomes the new wage."], ["No additional WML increase: Should the government decide to increase the Statutory Minimum Wage extra on top of the semi-annual increase, this increase will not be passed on throughout the entire wage structure."], ["Extra wage structure (performance-dependent part): In the extra wage structure (the performance-dependent part), each scale receives an extra step. In connection with this extra step, no more agreements are made in the CAO about a wage increase for those above scale."], ["Set-off of advance wage increases: Wage increases given in anticipation of CAO wage increases in 2024 and 2025 may be set off against the subsequent CAO wage increase. This also applies to wage increases given that are higher than the CAO increases."], ["Crisis clause: Employers who are in financial difficulties may, if the unions involved in this CAO agree, temporarily convert the wage increases in 2024 into free time or extra leave days. However, the wage can never fall below the Statutory Minimum Wage. If this free time/extra leave days cannot be taken before 1 January 2025, the value of the deferred wage increase, reduced by the free time already taken, must still be paid out in December 2024. Employer and employee can also agree that untaken leave will be retained as leave."], ["Wage tables: The wage tables are in appendix 1b. Unless otherwise stated, the wage table for Mode applies. Appendix 1a contains the implementation agreements and the system for granting wage increases. The employer determines whether performance-dependent remuneration is applied. If a works council is present, the employer must consult with it. If it is decided to remunerate performance-dependently, the performance appraisal system applies to all employees aged 21 and older. The system must at least meet established criteria (see appendix 1d). Examples of simple to more complex performance appraisal systems are offered for the sector. Performance-dependent remuneration is possible after a one-year appraisal cycle has taken place."], ["Wage by age: Wage increase due to age takes effect on the first day of the payment period in which the employee has their birthday. This applies to employees who start employment before their 21st birthday. This also remains the date on which an experience year is granted after their 21st birthday."], ["Experience year: For employees who are 21 years or older upon commencement of employment: wage increase due to reaching an experience year takes effect on the first day of the payment period in which the employee has one year of experience in the function. If a company applies the system of performance-dependent remuneration, the experience year is replaced by the performance appraisal increase. A company can choose to grant the performance appraisal increase at a fixed time of the year - this fixed time then applies to all employees - which means that the increase date does not have to coincide with the employee reaching the experience year. In that case, every employee with at least 6 months of experience in a function must be eligible for the performance appraisal increase. Employees who would receive an experience year in 2024 based on the foregoing are not entitled to this step increase in 2024. Employees who start employment in 2024 and would therefore receive a step increase in 2025 are not entitled to this step increase in 2025."], ["Promotion: If an employee was promoted in 2023 and their wage on 1 January 2024 is lower than it would have been if the promotion had taken place in 2024, they will receive the step and salary they would have been entitled to for a promotion in 2024 as of 1 January 2024."], ["Placement in another function: If the employee is placed in another function, they will be classified in the function group belonging to that function as of the date of appointment in that function. From that day on, they will receive the corresponding wage, unless a functional probationary period has been agreed. When placed in a higher function group, the employee is entitled to at least the same wage."], ["Allowance for temporary replacement in higher function (Jewelers or Perfumeries): Employees working at jewelers or perfumeries who replace employees in a higher function are entitled, during the period of replacement, to an allowance of 15% of the amount at zero function years in the scale in which the function to be replaced is classified. However, the temporary new salary (salary plus 15%) cannot exceed the salary of the functionary being replaced. There is no entitlement to this allowance for replacement due to vacation and when the replacement lasts less than one calendar month."], ["Wage for public holidays: The employee is entitled to continued payment of wages on a public holiday (for definition see art. 2 of this CAO) if he would normally perform work on that day. In the case of an irregular work pattern, this can be determined if the employee has performed work on the day in question in at least 8 of the preceding 13 weeks. In determining this, all realized hours (see article 2) count as worked days. If the 8 out of 13 criterion is met, the wage will be paid based on the average number of hours worked on those days. If the employee performs work on the public holiday, the allowance scheme of article 7.3 also applies."], ["Company Emergency Response (BHV) allowance: Employees who, at the request of the employer, also serve as company emergency responders (BHV-er) in addition to their regular duties, receive an allowance of € 75 gross/year for obtaining or renewing the certificate."], ["Internship allowance: Interns at MBO and HBO level receive an internship allowance of € 250 gross per month. Interns doing a 'snuffelstage' (orientation internship) do not receive an internship allowance."], ["MODULE SCHOENENDETAILHANDEL - Diploma allowance: If an employee follows one of the training/courses mentioned below and obtains the corresponding diploma/certificate, he/she receives a monthly allowance on top of his/her guaranteed wage. The employer has met this criterion if and insofar as this has been established as such by the implementing body (Tax Authorities), which is responsible for classifying companies into sectors for social insurance, AND d. the employer is not part of a group that is directly or by generally binding declaration bound by the CAO of one of the branches of industry as mentioned in Article 3 AND e. the employer is not a jointly agreed labor pool AND f. the employer did not fall under the (generally binding provisions of the) CAO Vervroegd Uittreden Metaal en Technische Bedrijfstakken on December 1, 1999.",
      "For the application of parts a. and b., employees, or the number of working hours of employees, whose function is entirely dedicated to the business activity 'making available' such as administration and mediation, are disregarded."

      "Employee Participation Body (Article 5)",
      "An employer with 50 or more employees will establish a works council pursuant to the Wet op de ondernemingsraden.",
      "An employer with 10 or more employees but fewer than 50, where no works council has been established, may establish an employee representation body. At the request of the majority of the employees working for the employer, the employer establishes the employee representation body.",
      "An employer with fewer than 10 employees, where no works council has been established, may establish an employee representation body.",
      "An employer with 10 or more employees but fewer than 50, where no works council or employee representation body has been established, will establish an employee meeting as described in Article 35b of the Wet op de ondernemingsraden, insofar as this Act applies to these companies."

      "Merger, Closure and Reorganization (Article 9)",
      "The provisions in the following paragraphs of this article apply to companies where at least 20 persons are regularly employed.",
      "The employer who is conducting merger discussions, or intends to close the company entirely or partially and/or to significantly reorganize the workforce, or has other plans that will have a significant negative impact on employment, will inform the employers' organizations (w.v.) and employees' organizations (v.v.) thereof. The employer will strive to prevent collective dismissals as much as possible.",
      "The notification referred to in paragraph 2 of this article must take place as soon as it can be expected that the possible merger, closure, and/or reorganization may proceed.",
      "Together with the aforementioned notification, the employer will also communicate the reasons that led to his decision, as well as what (social) consequences he expects, if and insofar as those consequences are already foreseeable at that time.",
      "Together with the w.v. and the v.v., the employer will, as soon as it becomes necessary, pay attention to: - the time at which the employee participation body or the employee delegation will be informed for advice; - the time and manner in which all personnel will be informed; - the question of whether and to what extent provisions can be made to prevent, eliminate, or reduce any adverse consequences for employees as much as possible.",

      "Peace Obligation (Article 76a)",
      "The employees' organizations involved in this CAO commit themselves, during the term of this agreement, not to apply any strikes aimed at changing the provisions of this agreement, nor to tolerate their application by their members.",
      "If one or more employees' organizations (v.v.) intend to strike or take other actions, not falling under paragraph 1, that will hinder the normal functioning of the company, those v.v. will notify the Stichting Vakraad and the employer concerned. After this notification, consultation will take place as soon as possible between the parties to this agreement regarding the intention, the possible consequences, and the possibilities of avoiding the strike and other actions. The intention to strike or take other actions will not be carried out within four weeks after notification to the Stichting Vakraad.",
      "The employers' organizations (w.v.) and their members will not apply lockouts to the members of the v.v. during the term of this agreement, as long as the v.v. or the members of the v.v. do not apply strikes or other actions at one or more employers.",

      "Scope of Application (Article 77)",
      "This agreement applies to employers in the industry and employees in the branch of metal processing, including: 3D printing, laying, assembling, constructing, disassembling, turning, enameling, extruding, forging, milling, casting, repairing, honing, boring, laser cladding, (laser) welding, lapping, mounting, maintaining (including preventive), designing*, developing*, pressing, flattening, assembling, demolishing, forging, cutting, drawing, manufacturing, (spark) machining, rolling, sawing of metal (including: aluminum, tinplate, bronze, copper, lead, brass, steel, tin, iron, zinc, and alloys or composites thereof) or metal objects, all in the broadest sense of the word, such as: apparatus, appendages, automatons, automobiles, statues, petrol pumps, irrigation systems, lightning conductors, canned goods, bolts, safes, bridges, pipes, capsules, containers not being car bodies, wire, wire nails, drive mechanisms, electrodes, mesh, motorized bicycles, tools, fireplaces, instruments (including optical apparatus), blinds, stoves, boilers, prams, rivets, buttons, crown caps, machines, mattresses, dies, meters (e.g., gas, electricity, water, and taximeters), furniture, nuts, engines, motorcycles, musical instruments, parts, ovens, windows, reservoirs, rolling gates, rolling stock, roller shutters, bicycles, skates, ships (all vessels however named and of whatever nature), screws, sliding and ornamental gates, closures, stamps, steam boilers, tanks, appliances, tubes, timepieces, tools (including power and work tools, agricultural machinery, tractors and implements) and sun blinds.",
      "Also applies to: monitoring*, designing*, developing*, manufacturing of apparatus, installations, substances, appliances, objects, etc., regardless of the nature of the material, that emit, store, use, measure, convert, transmit, switch, transform, consume, distribute, generate or make perceptible electrical energy or its components, such as products serving to measure, mutate, switch, transform and generate electrical power; electric motors, electric household and industrial appliances with and without electric motive power, electric ovens, stoves, apparatus for electric welding and accumulators; products serving for underground transport of electrical power (underground cable), and insulated wire; installation material, including fuses; apparatus and instruments in the field of telephony, telegraphy and other telecommunication purposes; lighting, gas discharge tubes for high and low voltages and electron tubes; dry batteries; radio, radar, television, transmitting, receiving and all other electronic equipment, including electro-medical appliances and instruments and computers.",
      "Also applies to: steel blasting and/or grit blasting of metal objects; galvanizing and/or tinning, insofar as this is not done by galvanotechnical means.",
      "The aforementioned branches of industry (a to d) exclusively include companies where, taking into account the normal number of working hours applicable in the industry, work is generally performed for less than 1,200 hours per week by employees in service with that company.",
      "A company that, due to the number of working hours of its employees, belongs to the branches of industry mentioned under a to d, belongs to the metal industry if the said number of working hours per week in that company, taking into account the normal number of working hours applicable in the industry, has amounted to at least 1,200, 2,000, or 3,000 respectively, for an uninterrupted period of 3, 2, or 1 year respectively, calculated from January 1 of any year, after the end of that period, with due observance of what is stated below under g.",
      "The company referred to in sub f belongs to the metal industry from the first day of the next calendar year commencing after the periods mentioned in sub f.",
      "Companies whose business operations exclusively or primarily belong to the branches of industry mentioned under a to d to which the criterion of the number of employees applicable until January 1, 1985, applies and which are registered with the Sector Metaal en Technische Bedrijfstakken (formerly Bedrijfsvereniging voor de Metaalnijverheid) but where, on or before the said date, taking into account that criterion, affiliation with the Sector Metaalindustrie or Sector Elektrotechnische Industrie (formerly together the Bedrijfsvereniging voor de Metaalindustrie en Electrotechnische Industrie) should have taken place, continue to belong to the metalworking industry.",
      "In case of legal succession of a company as referred to above in sub f and sub h, for the application of what is stated in sub f and sub h, it is assumed that there is the same affiliation.",
      "If a company as referred to in sub h, in the context of what is determined by or pursuant to the Regeling Wfsv (Wet financiering sociale verzekeringen) of the Minister of Social Affairs and Employment and the State Secretary of Finance of December 2, 2005, Directorate Social Insurance, No. SV/F&W/05/96420, published in the Staatscourant number 242 of December 13, 2005, transfers to the Sector Metaalindustrie or Sector Elektrotechnische Industrie (formerly together the Bedrijfsvereniging voor de Metaalindustrie en Electrotechnische Industrie), that company belongs to Metalectro from the same date.",
      "The Commissie Werkingssfeer oversees the application of the rules stated in sub e to j regarding the classification and transition of companies.",
      "Regardless of the number of working hours during which work is generally performed per week by employees in service with those companies, companies in which exclusively or primarily one or more of the following businesses are carried out do not belong to the metalworking industry: a. the rolling of steel; b. the iron and steel foundry industry; c. the manufacturing and/or repairing of aircraft; d. the manufacturing and/or repairing of elevators.",
      "Under manufacturing in the preceding, assembling, mounting, and combining from parts obtained from third parties are also understood.",
      "This agreement also applies to employers in the industry and employees in the galvanotechnical industry, which means: applying metal deposits to objects, oxidizing, or polishing metals by electrochemical or other means.",
      "This agreement also applies to employers in the industry and employees in the hand and machine engraving industry, which means: the business of engraving in metal or other materials.",
      "This agreement also applies to employers in the industry and employees in the model making industry, which means: the manufacturing, repairing, and modifying of casting models, molding plates, and molds.",
      "This agreement also applies to employers in the industry and employees in the business of lacquering, enameling, grinding, and/or polishing metal, applying coatings of plastics or ceramics to metal; repairing sewing machines; manufacturing and/or repairing rowing boats, small sailboats, canoes, ships (all vessels however named and of whatever nature) made of wood and/or plastics.",
      "This agreement also applies to employers in the industry and employees in the business of manufacturing products and/or parts of products from plastic, including: lighters, appendages, bolts, capsules, household utensils, installation material, cabinets, frames, crown caps, nuts, nameplates, traffic signs, products for the construction industry, windows, reservoirs, gears, tanks, and silos, with the exception of the exclusive or primary manufacturing of plastic as raw material and/or synthetic fiber and products of synthetic fiber.",
      "This agreement also applies, if paragraph 1 of this article is not applicable, to employers in the industry and employees in the business of roller shutters, awnings, and sun protection, which means: designing*, developing*, applying, assembling, repairing, leasing, storing, trading, renting, manufacturing of indoor sun protection and/or outdoor sun protection and/or closures, regardless of the purpose and/or use.",
      "Under 'designing' and/or 'developing' is understood, starting from a program of requirements, the conversion of this program into a technical specification, which also includes sketches, blueprints, or prototypes, etc.",
      "Under 'monitoring' is understood the (remote or otherwise) influencing, controlling, and/or maintaining (including preventive) the operation of the above-mentioned products manufactured by the employee(s) of the employer."

      "Salary Payment (Article 31)",
      "Salary payment takes place exclusively either per month or per four-week period. Salary is understood to mean the agreed fixed remuneration, including the personal allowance as referred to in Article 36a and comparable fixed salary components (excluding any allowances, bonuses, expense reimbursements, and the like) that are due per month or per four-week period by the employer.",
      "The payment of salary and any shift allowance takes place no later than the last working day of the month or four-week period for which this salary is due.",
      "The payment of any allowances, bonuses, expense reimbursements, and the like, and of recoverable advances, takes place no later than the last working day of the following month or four-week period as referred to in paragraph 2, unless otherwise stipulated in this CAO.",
      "For travel, accommodation, and other costs, advances will be provided if the employee requests it.",
      "Work hours, travel, accommodation, and other costs not properly accounted for by the employee will not be paid.",
      "The employer provides a written calculation of the payments referred to in paragraph 3 upon request.",
      "The employer provides the employee with a written statement of what he has received from the employment in that year and of the deductions made thereon, within two months after the end of the calendar year or upon termination of employment."

      "Salary Increase in Connection with Age (Article 34)",
      "Increases related to age are granted from the payment period in which the employee's birthday falls.",
      "The foregoing does not apply if a different, uniform regulation was in force for all employees, or is established in consultation with the employee participation body or the employee delegation."

    [
      "Salary Increase in Connection with Function Years (Article 35)",
      "Increases based on function years are granted once a year, but no later than in the payment period in which a new function year begins, until the maximum number of function years in the relevant salary group has been reached.",
      "If a different, uniform regulation was in force for all employees, or is established in consultation with the employee participation body or the employee delegation, a function year increase may be granted in a period of up to 6 months after, and with retroactive effect to, the payment period in which a new function year begins."

    [
      "Change of Function (Article 36)",
      "An employee who will perform a function classified in a higher salary group will maintain at least the same salary.",
      "The provision in paragraph 1 applies analogously to an employee who will perform a different function classified in the same salary group.",
      "An employee who will perform a function classified in a lower salary group will continue to receive the salary earned in the previous function for at least three payment periods. Subsequently, he may be classified into the new salary group, either gradually or not.",
      "In derogation from the provisions of paragraph 3, when an employee of 55 years or older is classified into a lower salary group, the difference between the old and new salary will be paid in the form of an allowance. Salary increases will only be calculated on the new salary. The allowance will remain unchanged. This provision does not apply if the employee requests placement in a function in a lower function group.",
      "For an employee who is retrained upon commencing employment, no salary scale applies during the first three payment periods. Retraining is understood to mean training to regain wholly or partially lost competence in performing a specific function that the employee to be trained performed.",
      "For an employee who is reskilled upon commencing employment, no salary scale applies during the first six payment periods, with the understanding that this period of six to twelve payment periods is extended if reskilling takes place for a function classified in one of the salary groups higher than D.",
      "For an employee who, upon commencing employment, will receive further training to acquire more professional knowledge or skills for performing his function, no salary scale will apply during the first three payment periods if the further training takes place during daily working hours.",
      "An employee already in service who, at the employer's request, follows a course for re-, re- or further training, as mentioned in paragraphs 5 to 7, will not suffer a salary reduction during the course.",
      "The employer may agree with an employee who is undergoing re-, re- or further training that the employment will not be terminated by either party within one year after the training has been completed, successfully or not. Such an agreement must be made in writing. Such an agreement may also stipulate that the employer will place the employee in the function for which he has been re-, re- or further trained, or that the employee will accept the said function.",
      "An employee who terminates the employment before the time referred to in paragraph 9 is liable for damages. The compensation is limited to the salary, increased by the costs that the employer may have incurred for the employee in connection with his participation in the course, such as books, travel expenses, etc."

    [
      "Salary Consequences of FC-Handboek Implementation (Article 36a)",
      "Employees who have not yet reached the 'vakvolwassen leeftijd': 1a. If an employee who has not yet reached the 'vakvolwassen leeftijd' is classified into a higher function group, he will be classified into the salary group appropriate to his classification and age. His new salary must be at least equal to his current salary. 1b. If an employee who has not yet reached the 'vakvolwassen leeftijd' is classified into a lower function group, he will be classified into the salary group appropriate to his classification and age. The difference between the current salary and the table salary corresponding to his age and new salary group will be granted in the form of a personal allowance. In the event that there is no corresponding age, the employee will be scaled in with fictitious function years such that the table salary is equal to the current salary. If the current salary appears to be between the table salaries corresponding to two consecutive function years, the salary corresponding to the table salary of the lower function year will be granted. The difference between the current salary and the table salary belonging to his classification will be granted in the form of a personal allowance. Collective salary increases will be granted on the personal allowance. Individual salary increases will be offset against the personal allowance.",
      "Employees who have reached the 'vakvolwassen leeftijd': 2a. If an employee who has reached the 'vakvolwassen leeftijd' is classified into a higher function group, he will be classified into the salary group appropriate to his classification, such that he receives a fictitious number of function years whereby his new salary is at least equal to his current salary. If the employee has a salary lower than the table salary at 0 function years, the salary will be increased to be equal to the table salary belonging to 0 function years. If the employee has a salary that is between the table salaries of two consecutive function years of his new salary group, the salary will be increased to be equal to the table salary belonging to the next higher function year. 2b. If an employee who has reached the 'vakvolwassen leeftijd' is classified into a lower function group, he will be classified into the salary group appropriate to his classification, such that his new salary is equal to his current salary. If the employee's current salary falls between the table salaries of two consecutive function years, the salary corresponding to the table salary of the lower function year will be granted. The difference between the (lower) table salary and the current salary will be granted in the form of a personal allowance. Collective salary increases will be granted on the personal allowance. Individual salary increases will be offset against the personal allowance."

      "If the consultation between the employer and the employee participation body or the employee delegation does not lead to agreement, but the employer nevertheless wishes to make a decision as referred to in paragraph 1, he will seek advice from the bedrijfsraad, in which case the employer and the employee participation body or the employee delegation must act in accordance with the advice given by the bedrijfsraad."

      "Union Contribution (Article 39)",
      "The employee may submit a request to the employer for a reduction of the gross wage in December equal to the costs paid by him in the relevant calendar year for membership of an employees' organization, as further determined in the Reglement Aanvulling arbeidsovereenkomst. The employer may grant this request, in exchange for an expense reimbursement equal to the aforementioned paid membership costs. If the request is not granted, the employer must pay for a study day in which the employee participates and which day is demonstrably organized by one of the employees' organizations (v.v.). The employee must submit a request in time so that the choice for the study day or the aforementioned reimbursement can be taken into account."

      "When an exchange involves a conversion of time into money or vice versa, the exchange rate is 0.607% of the monthly salary per hour as mentioned in Article 31 CAO (0.658% per hour of the four-week salary).",
      "For settlement, the employee can choose from the following options: a. The costs of buying free time pursuant to paragraph 4, calculated based on the exchange rate from paragraph 7, are deducted from allowances to be paid to the employee, such as, and insofar as applicable, overtime allowance, shift allowance, holiday allowance, day window allowance, all insofar as the allowance is not included in the salary as referred to in Article 31 CAO. In case of payment for the sale of free time, the payment takes place in the period(s) in which work is performed on these days. The funds obtained from the sale of free time can also be used for tax-facilitated schemes. b. The costs or benefits, calculated based on the exchange rate from paragraph 7, associated with the exchange for the employee, are deducted from the salary payment or paid out in equal parts during the calendar year with the payment of the monthly salary or the four-week salary. c. Deducted amounts for which no leave could be taken in that quarter are then still paid out with the salary payment; extra paid out amounts for which no extra work could be performed in that quarter are then still deducted or not paid out. Employer and employee can make other agreements about this in consultation.",
      "1.
